The basic format's series_cap is 4 for everyone. A club reading the card would under-dress its skaters and believe a goaltender may play five or six games of a series.

### Low (87)

- **F-0-10** (Rule 0.6): A club's roster is settled by the draft and the placement ten minutes later, and the only movement left in the season is a trade or the signing of a waived player. *Correction:* 0.6.2, second sentence: "Every contract ends with its season and every player re-enters the following draft, so a club's roster is set by the draft and the placement that follows ten minutes later. After that it changes only by a trade between two clubs, by waiving a player or signing one who has been waived (Rule 2.2, Rule 2.3), by the automatic placement of late registrants as depth until the movement deadline (Rule 2.8), and by a player's departure under Rule 1.1."
- **F-0-11** (Rule 0.4): Drafting every registered player on the published Saturday ...; overall ratings are compiled from regular-season play. *Correction:* 0.4.2: change "Drafting every registered player on the published Saturday" to "Giving every registered player a club on the published Saturday", and change "Overall ratings are compiled from regular-season play" to "Overall ratings are compiled from regular-season and playoff play".
- **F-0-12** (Rule 0.2, 0.9 (with 2.8.2)): The only precondition for a new season is that each club's front office is in place; the next draft may be held as soon as every club's front office is seated. *Correction:* 0.2.2: "... the only precondition for a new season is that each club has its Owner and General Manager in place." 0.9.1: "... and the next draft may be held as soon as every club has an Owner and a General Manager (Rule 2.8)."
- **F-0-13** (Rule 0.5 (with 2.8.8)): Nobody drafts for a club. *Correction:* 0.5.2: replace "Nobody drafts for a club." with "No selection is made for a club automatically."
- **F-0-14** (Rule 0.7): A forfeit in which no player took the ice counts toward no player's weekly six on either club. *Correction:* 0.7.2: change "counts toward no player's weekly six on either club" to "counts toward no player's weekly limit on either club".
- **F-0-15** (Rule 0.4 (Appendix A)): Pre-season games count toward a player's overall rating but never toward the standings. *Correction:* 0.4.full.2, last sentence: "Pre-season games count toward neither a player's overall rating nor the regular-season standings or the playoff race."
- **F-0-16** (Rule 0.6 (Appendix A)): Every registered player still without a club, undrafted or short of five games, is placed ten minutes after the draft. *Correction:* 0.6.full.1: change "undrafted, or short of five games" to "undrafted, or short of three games".
- **F-0-17** (Rule 0.4 (Appendix A)): Returning players and club management are exempt from the three-game pre-season requirement. *Correction:* 0.4.full.3, second sentence: "Returning players (a player drafted or rostered in an earlier season, or with five (5) or more league games on record) and club management are exempt and may play as much or as little of the pre-season as they wish." (Alternatively drop the five-game test from the code.)
- **F-0-18** (Rule 0.1 (site copy)): The number of draft rounds is a season setting the commissioner publishes. *Correction:* Change the caption to "The round count is the season's published setting."
- **F-1-11** (Rule 1.3): Inside its own club room a club's management may mention whomever it needs, as often as it needs; the server's spam filters never block the front office there. *Correction:* Replace the last sentence with: "The server's spam filters never block a club's Owner, General Manager or Assistant General Manager, in any channel, and the mention filter does not apply to anyone posting inside a club's own room."
- **F-1-4** (Rule 1.1): A player is not eligible to appear in a CGHL game until the league office has confirmed their registration and placed them on a club. *Correction:* Replace the sentence with: "A player is not eligible to appear in a CGHL game until he has been drafted by a club or placed on one by the league office (Rule 2.8)."
- **F-1-5** (Rule 1.1): Every registered player is placed: by the draft, or by the league office in the placement that follows it (Rule 2.8). *Correction:* Replace the sentence with: "At the conclusion of the draft every registered player is placed, by the draft or by the league office in the placement that follows it (Rule 2.8), except a player whose registration the league office has declined or who is suspended or banned. A player who is later waived becomes a free agent under Rule 2.2 and is not placed again." The same qualification belongs in Rule 2.8.8 ("no registered player is left without a club").
- **F-1-6** (Rule 1.1): If he does not rejoin, his registration is revoked and archived exactly as it would have been above, he is removed from the club's roster or training camp, and the spot is free. *Correction:* System: add the same site notification to the player that 1.1.4 gives, and record the removal under its own transaction type rather than 'waive'. Book: replace "exactly as it would have been above" with "as it would have been above" and add: "The removal is recorded in the league's transaction log. A player removed under this paragraph is not a waived player under Rule 2.2: his registration ends and he does not enter the pool of free agents."
- **F-1-8** (Rule 1.1): Registering after the full-format deadline places the player on a club automatically under Rule 2.2. *Correction:* Before any full-format season, change the full-format notice in auto_assign_latecomers to describe a post-draft placement at the league minimum under Rule 2.2 (full). No book change.
- **F-1-9** (Rule 1.3): New accounts must also have been in the server ten (10) minutes before they can post at all. *Correction:* Replace the sentence with: "A new account is also held by the server's own verification setting until the league's automatic sync gives it its first role, which happens within about two (2) minutes of joining." If the commissioner wants the full ten minutes, the system change is instead to withhold the 'Not Signed Up' role until an account has been in the server ten minutes.
- **F-2-37** (Rule 2.6): Full format: Owner $0, GM and AGM $3,000,000 each; the 14-round class brings a club within $750,000 of the cap; all three seats before the draft. *Correction:* Book: state in 2.6.full.3 that management values are season settings published before the draft, drop the 'within $750,000' clause from 2.8.full.5, and align 2.6.full.1 and 2.8.full.2 with 2.8.2 (Owner and GM required, AGM may follow). System: set the column defaults to 0/0/2,000,000.

## Part 4. Settled by the book: fixes, not questions

The auditors asked these, but the second check found the book already answers them. The system (or the site's copy) should follow the book.

- **Q-0-2** (Rule 0.1.3 (with 2.1.1, 2.8.1, 8.3.4, 10.1.3)). Once a season's settings are published, which of them may the commissioner still change during the season, and should the system lock the others? *Today:* The roster setting is not freely editable. The DB trigger guard_season_format_trg (public.guard_season_format()) refuses any seasons.roster_max other than format_rules(format).roster_max, and the editor field is readonly (part_live.js:11821). The composition (quota, lines, flex) exists only in format_rules, so a season cannot set it at all except by switching format, and a mid-season format switch is not blocked. public.season_rules(uuid) overlays only draft_rounds and playoff_min_gp. After picks exist, generate_draft_board refuses to rebuild. playoff_min_gp stays editable at any time in Control Center > Seasons (part_live.js:11820, saved at :11948) with no DB lock. position_locks is a season column with no editor field. RLS 'commissioner writes seasons' lets a commissioner update any column directly. *Why it is settled:* This is not a genuine decision. 0.1.3, 2.1.1, 2.8.1 and 8.3.4 already say the published roster, round count and playoff floor are 'binding for its duration'. The position lock and minimum service are not among 0.1.3's three binding figures. 2.1.3 already records the position lock being lifted by announcement on 26 Sep 2026, and 10.1.3 records minimum service at zero. What remains is enforcement, a fix rather than a question: the playoff floor (and a format switch) can still be changed after the draft. Option A's 'lock roster_max' is already done by guard_season_format. Option B would rewrite the book to match an editor capability, which is not a real conflict in the rules.
- **Q-1-2** (Rule 1.3.2 / 3.3.1). Should the site enforce that only a club's management may file a protest or a reschedule request, or should the complaint form stay open to every member with the office applying Rule 1.3.2 by hand? *Today:* action_requests allows only the types complaint, appeal, trade_request and position_change (action_requests_type_check). Its INSERT policy ar_insert checks only profile_id = auth.uid(), so any signed-in member can file a complaint (part_live.js:8026, 8323). There is no protest or reschedule-request type, and a complaint is not tied to a game: it carries a subject list plus an optional target member. Reschedules happen only through the operations desk / Control Center (ops_reschedule_game, part9_staffdesks.js:577; part_live.js:11678), acting on requests arranged outside the site. *Why it is settled:* The book already settles the rule: 1.3.2 says protests and reschedule requests must come from management to be considered filed, and 3.3.1 requires written agreement of both managements. The office applying that by hand is a MANUAL-verdict implementation choice, not a rule decision. The real gap is different: 'protest' appears only in 1.3.2, and no procedure or venue for filing one exists anywhere in the book or the system. That gap is worth raising as its own item.
- **Q-1-4** (Rule 1.3.1 / 7.2.2). Are disciplinary sanctions to be posted in a member-facing Discord channel, or delivered only to the member concerned and recorded on the site? *Today:* The public.notify_suspension() trigger sends the member a site notification (create_notification) and queues a Discord DM (discord_dms, kind 'suspension') when he has a discord_id. It also posts to the staff 'casework' channel through notify_staff_ch. Nothing posts to a member-facing channel, and no grep hit in netlify/functions or bot posts suspensions. The RLS policy 'suspensions are league record' lets anon and authenticated read every non-warning suspension, and the site shows them from the suspensions table (part_live.js:594, 859). Warnings stay private to the member, commissioners and officiating. *Why it is settled:* 7.2.2 already settles delivery: 'A member is told of a sanction at the moment it is imposed, on the site and, where he has linked his Discord account, by direct message.' A DM is delivery through Discord, so 1.3.1 is satisfied. Public channel posting is not required by the book, and the system matches. At most, 1.3.1 and 7.6.1 ('sanction being posted') need an editorial wording fix. This is not a decision.
- **Q-2-10** (Rule 2.7). Is the separation of Rule 2.7.1 absolute, or may a commissioner grant documented exceptions? *Today:* role_conflict_exemptions holds 3 rows. start_next_season(uuid) deletes them all once the season number is 2 or higher, and is_role_exempt() reads them in guard_mgmt_nominee, decide_staff_application, waitlist_admit, apply_application_decision and set_staff_profile. None of the 3 exempt members holds a club seat now, so the exemptions are in use for nobody. Exactly one league-office member sits in a club seat today, and that member is Media-only staff, which [2.7.2] allows. set_member_role(uuid,user_role,text) has no Rule 2.7 check; its source comment says the rule is 'enforced as league policy, not a hard block' (that is a code comment, not a pg comment on the function). set_team_manager and _set_team_seat have no Rule 2.7 check either. *Why it is settled:* Already settled. [2.7.1] is absolute apart from Media. The commissioner ruled on 2026-07-16 (memory/role-conflict-rules.md) to grandfather three members for Season 1 and apply the rule in full from Season 2, and start_next_season lapses the exemptions as that ruling requires. Nobody holds both roles on an exemption today, so 'exceptions TODAY in practice' overstates it. What remains is a fix: either record the now-moot Season 1 grandfathering in [2.7.1] or leave it out, and optionally add the Rule 2.7 check to the office seating path (_set_team_seat/set_team_manager) and to set_member_role so the office cannot create a conflict unnoticed. [2.7.4] already provides the only discretionary exception (cooling-off, when a club would otherwise fold).
- **Q-2-15** (Rule 2.2). May a club sign an unrostered registrant who was never waived, such as a late registrant awaiting placement? *Today:* public.sign_free_agent(uuid,bigint) accepts any non-declined, unrostered, unsuspended current-season registration with no waived_at requirement. It checks only fa_open, moves_locked (non-commissioner), under-contract and the roster_max total. The free-agent board (part_live.js:13200-13204 via CG.poolState, 6096-6098) lists only the 'free_agent' state: waived players, plus isReturning players, of whom there are none in Season 1. Undrafted players and late sign-ups show as 'Awaiting placement' or 'Late sign-up' and are not listed. Right now 10 registrations are unrostered and all 10 are waived. *Why it is settled:* Not a genuine decision: the book already answers it. 2.2.1 says every late registrant 'is placed on a club by the league office' and forbids offering a contract to an unrostered player, and 2.2.3 says 'The one signing available to a club is of such a player' (a waived one). This is a database fix: sign_free_agent should refuse a registration without waived_at. The gap is narrow in practice because latecomer-assign runs every 5 minutes, but it also covers players removed without a waiver (office removal, Discord departure). Separately, sign_free_agent checks the roster total, not room in the position group as 2.2.3 says.
- **Q-2-21** (Rule 2.8). After a selection is reversed once the draft has concluded, does the club re-select, or is the selection replaced at random? *Today:* After draft_conclude sets draft_state to 'complete', public.draft_reverse_pick(uuid) deletes the player's roster spot and returns his registration to pending. It sets the pick back to used=false, skipped=false, then calls _draft_advance. That leaves the status 'complete' but bumps updated_at, which restarts the ten-minute window in distribute_unproven_rookies. The club cannot re-select: public.draft_make_pick requires status live or paused, and no function reopens a completed draft (draft_resume needs 'paused'). The next placement run uses _place_for_unused_picks, which counts used=false picks. It seats a random fitting player at $750,000, not at the round's salary. The reversed player can himself be placed as depth in the second pass. *Why it is settled:* The book settles this. Rule 2.8.8 says unused selections are filled at random after conclusion and 'No club selects', and says the ten-minute period exists so the office may still reverse a selection. Rule 2.8.6 says a selection unused at conclusion is replaced under paragraph 8. No 'reopen' power exists in the book or the system. At most this is an editorial fix: add 'a selection reversed after conclusion' to the list in 2.8.8's first pass.
- **Q-2-3** (Rule 2.1). Does the weekly Wednesday-to-Friday freeze cover trades, waivers and waiver signings, or only call-ups and send-downs? *Today:* public.roster_freeze_at(timestamptz) is read only by the trigger public.guard_squad_move() (BEFORE UPDATE OF squad on roster_spots), which refuses a squad change between Wed 7:30 PM and Fri 11:59 PM ET unless the caller is a commissioner or trusted_writer(). accept_trade(uuid), sign_free_agent(uuid,bigint) and waive_player(uuid) never consult it, so trades, waivers and signings all go through during the window. A player moved by trade is set to squad 'pro' by reset_squad_on_team_change(). Because move_player's UPDATE does not name the squad column, guard_squad_move does not fire, so a camp player traded mid-freeze lands on the active roster. A signing is placed by place_new_roster_spot(): active if there is room, otherwise camp. *Why it is settled:* This is settled already. The commissioner's announcement in sql/2026-09-26-opening-week-rule-changes.sql says the freeze stops teams moving players 'between training camp and the active roster' and 'applies to both call-ups and send-downs'. [2.1.4] already limits the freeze to call-ups and send-downs ('They are restricted in one way only'), and the system matches both. What needs fixing is one sentence in [2.1.4]: 'The week's games are played against the roster a club held when the week's first puck dropped.' It is false, because trades and waived-player signings still land mid-week, and [2.3.1] makes a traded player eligible from his new club's next game. Treat this as a wording fix, not a question for the commissioner. Recommend (a)'s wording.
- **Q-2-4** (Rule 2.1). Does the freeze follow the calendar (every Wednesday 7:30 PM to Friday midnight ET, year round) or the game week (from the hour availability is due for that week's first night)? *Today:* public.roster_freeze_at() tests only the Eastern day of the week and the time (Wed at or after 19:30, all of Thu and Fri). It never reads the schedule, so it runs every week of the year, including weeks with no games and the playoffs. CG.rosterFreeze in part_live.js:150 mirrors it. All six Season 1 regular-season weeks open on a Wednesday at 21:00 ET (games table), and holiday weeks are skipped whole rather than shifted (memory/season-holidays.md, [0.7.1], [3.1.1]). *Why it is settled:* Not a genuine decision. [2.1.4] already says 'each week from Wednesday at 7:30 PM Eastern Time', which is the calendar reading. The clause 'the hour availability is due under Rule 5.1' matches that time in every week the schedule generator can produce, because nights are always Wed/Thu/Fri and a holiday skips the whole week. The two readings differ only in weeks with no games, where freezing squad moves costs nothing. A side point: [5.1.1] says 'a holiday-shifted week keeps the same rule against its own first night'. That describes a case the generator never produces (weeks are skipped, not shifted) and should get its own editorial fix. Adding (a)'s clarifying clause would be harmless.
- **Q-2-9** (Rule 2.1). Should roster size and composition, a camp limit and minimum service be true per-season settings, or fixed by the season's format? *Today:* public.season_rules(uuid) is format_rules(season_format) with only draft_rounds and playoff_min_gp overlaid from the seasons row. The trigger guard_season_format() refuses any roster_max that differs from format_rules(format).roster_max. camp_max() and min_service_gp() read season_rules, so the camp limit, roster composition (quota, lines, flex) and minimum service are format constants. position_locks is different: it is a real per-season column on seasons (read by position_locks_on), not part of season_rules. *Why it is settled:* Not a genuine decision. The rules in force are the same whichever way it is answered; only the mechanism differs. The commissioner's v2.50 instruction (memory/rulebook-register-and-season-settings.md) chose 'season settings' wording for the book and kept the format table as the mechanism, with changes made by editing format_rules. The book's 'the commissioner shall determine' is met by designating the format or editing the table. This should be an editorial fix, such as (b)'s wording, plus the pinned rulebook tests. Note that the 'season settings' list in [10.1.3] mixes true per-season columns (draft rounds, playoff floor, position lock) with format constants (composition, camp limit, minimum service). Overlaps Q-2-8 on minimum service.
- **Q-3-1** (Rule 3.2.4). May a member of league staff who is not involved in the game waive (lift) a ten-minute lateness forfeit, as Rule 3.2.4 says? *Today:* public.undo_forfeit (is_commissioner() or has_department('officiating')) and public.unforfeit_game (_is_stats_staff(): commissioner or statistics staff) reverse any forfeit, with no check that the actor is uninvolved in the game. public.log_game_incident returns 'Not waivable' for a ten-minute late start, and bot/incidents.mjs:24 and :183 post 'not waivable' / 'The ten-minute forfeit is not waivable' to the clubs; tools/incident-rulings.test.mjs:19 pins that wording. *Why it is settled:* This is not a genuine decision. [3.2.4] plainly says an uninvolved staff member may waive the forfeit, and this was a deliberate change: tools/rulebook-222.test.cjs:72 to 74 pins exactly that wording and asserts the old 'is a hard rule and is not waivable' text is gone. The v2.7 'forfeit never' ruling in memory/roster-ruleset-v27.md was superseded by v2.22. What remains is a fix: the stale 'not waivable' copy in log_game_incident, bot/incidents.mjs and its test, plus the missing involvement guard (F-3-1).
- **Q-4-4** (Rule 4.3). For an unfinished game whose continuation never arrives, should the league rule it automatically under 4.3.9 once a club plays someone else, or should the statistics desk rule it by hand after the window closes? *Today:* public.review_held_games(integer) runs from netlify/functions/discord-sync.js:2459 (every 2 minutes, p_grace_hours 3) and, once a fixture's scheduled_at is 3 hours past, posts one 'A held game never finished' flag to the statistics department and logs held_game_stalled. The automatic lane exists but cannot reach a held game: closeOutAbandoned in netlify/functions/ingest-stats.js:1565 only scans games with status=eq.final, and public.forfeit_abandoned_game(uuid,uuid,text) refuses anything not final with a score. Since v3.18 a short first sitting stays 'scheduled' with no score (ingest-stats.js:783-798), so it is never ruled automatically. admin_audit holds zero abandoned_forfeit rows and zero held_game_stalled rows. *Why it is settled:* The book settles this. 4.3.9 says the league files the forfeit automatically, and the v2.94 record (sql/2026-09-23-abandoned-forfeit.sql) carries the commissioner's 2026-09-23 ruling ordering exactly that. v3.18 (sql/2026-09-25-hold-unfinished-games.sql) broke the automatic lane without meaning to: the sweep still filters on status=final, but held games are now 'scheduled'. 4.3.9 already covers the one worry v3.18 raised, a game that was really a resume, because the officials reverse the ruling. Treat this as a MISMATCH fix: point the sweep, and the forfeit_abandoned_game gate, at held games. The automatic lane is only reachable today for a FINAL game whose merged sittings are still short (see Q-4-10). Staff can rule a held game by hand only in two steps: file it through the club game stats desk merge (leagueMerge), then call forfeit_game with keep_result, which refuses a game that is not final.
- **Q-4-6** (Rule 4.5). With the position lock lifted, does a player's legal build follow the position he is dressed at for that game, or the position he is rostered at? *Today:* The EA payload DOES carry each player's build. Every player entry in the archived payloads (ea_ingest_log) has a numeric 'class' field beside 'position'; a sample showed class 15 for a defenseMen entry and class 2 for a rightWing entry. The importer neither stores nor checks it: there is no read of 'class' in ingest-stats.js or bot/ea-poll.mjs. Nothing in the system enforces Rule 4.5 builds, so either answer changes only the book. *Why it is settled:* The book already leans to A. 4.5.1 says every player 'dresses on a legal build for his position', and 4.5.4 ends 'No build outside this legal set may be dressed at that position', which ties the build to the position he is dressed at. Option B's claim that 4.5.4 supports the rostered position is not borne out, and option A's quotes are paraphrases, not the book's words. A one-sentence clarification is cheap, but no commissioner decision is needed. The class field could someday make build checks automatic, but that needs a mapping from class codes to builds, which does not exist.
- **Q-4-7** (Rule 4.5). Is a club that declines a Rule 4.5 restart ruled a 1-0 forfeit with no statistics, and does that forfeit count toward the dressed players' weekly limits? *Today:* Without keep_result, public.forfeit_game(uuid,uuid,text,boolean) (statistics staff only) deletes the game's game_stats, sets status final, and writes 1-0 to the non-forfeiting club with ea_match_id nulled. public.player_week_games(...) leaves out any forfeit-ruled game with no positive time_on_ice_seconds, so the game counts toward nobody's weekly limit. No separate Rule 4.5 path exists; staff use this tool. *Why it is settled:* The book settles it as option A. 4.5.5 says 'A club that declines to restart forfeits the game under Rule 3.2'. 3.2.2 says a Rule 3.2 forfeit is 1-0 with no individual statistics and counts toward no one's weekly total. 5.2.5 repeats that a Rule 3.2 forfeit counts for no one. The tool does exactly this. The only faint tension is 5.2.5's 'ice time is the test' wording, since a declined restart may have loaded, but deleting the stats also removes the ice time, so the result agrees.
- **Q-5-6** (Rule 5.1). When a player answered on time and later changes an answer after the deadline, is his week recorded as late? *Today:* stamp_availability (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE on availability) keeps first_submitted_at and carries old.late forward, so the flag is never cleared. It sets late = true on any INSERT or UPDATE after week_availability_deadline, even an update that changes nothing, unless the writer is a trusted writer, a commissioner or staff; those writers are exempt. notify_availability_submitted fires only when nights actually changes, and its club notice then says 'Submitted after the deadline (Rule 5.1)'. The late flag drives only labels (part6_hub.js:335, 475, 547); no rule logic reads it. *Why it is settled:* This is not a genuine decision. Rule 5.1.1 already says the system 'marks a post-deadline answer late', and a changed answer is a post-deadline answer. The site's own copy says so explicitly: 'a change after it is accepted at your club's discretion and recorded as late' (part6_hub.js:335) and the 'Changed after the 7:30 PM ET deadline' tooltip at part6_hub.js:547. The flag carries no consequence beyond a label. At most there is a wording fix for the club notice, which says 'Submitted after the deadline' about a player who first answered on time. The item's system_does claim missed the staff and trusted-writer exemption.
- **Q-7-1** (Rule 7.1.2, 7.7.3). Should a complaint (and the ruling that follows it) record where the conduct happened, in a game or in the league's Discord and other spaces, and be routed and sanctioned on that, or is filing under the 'Discord behavior' heading the rule that makes a matter a community one? *Today:* bot/staff-alerts.mjs route() sends a complaint to community only when its subject equals 'discord behavior' (DISCORD_SUBJECT, line 48). Every other heading in CG.COMPLAINT_SUBJECTS (part_live.js:8031), including 'Harassment or abuse' and 'Player conduct / toxicity', goes to officiating. action_requests has no venue column; its 'route' column is only commissioner or manager. suspensions records reason_codes (the 7.7 headings) but no venue. In suspend_player, the 3/6/9 ladder applies only to a holder of community ALONE, so a dual holder gets the 10-game/30-day ceiling. discipline_from_case never applies the ladder, even to a community-only moderator (up to 10 games, 30 days or a date). *Why it is settled:* The book already settles this: 7.1.2 and 7.7.1 route by where the conduct happened, and 7.7.3 says a dual holder acts under whichever rule fits the conduct. Routing by heading is a system MISMATCH to fix, not an open policy choice; how to capture the venue is an implementation detail. Also worth a fix: discipline_from_case lets community-only moderators bypass the Rule 7.7 3/6/9 scale.
- **Q-7-3** (Rule 7.1.2). Is a commissioner bound by the rule that no official may rule on a case he filed or a case about himself? *Today:* Every conflict check exempts commissioners. discipline_from_case refuses the filer, the target or the player himself only 'if not v_commish'. suspend_player bars self-suspension and disciplining a commissioner only for non-commissioners. assign_case's conflict check is wrapped in 'if not is_commissioner()'. RLS ar_update grants is_commissioner() outright. lift_suspension has no conflict check for anyone: an officiating member could lift a suspension against himself. *Why it is settled:* 7.1.2 already answers this. It says every official can see 'a complaint about a commissioner or a member of league staff', then that 'no official may rule on a case they filed or a case about themselves, so a complaint naming an official is decided by someone else'. With three commissioners, another commissioner can always decide, which is consistent with 7.2.1. The commissioner exemption is a MISMATCH, better filed as a fix. Separately, lift_suspension's missing self-check should be fixed whatever the answer.
- **Q-7-4** (Rule 7.1.2). Should the #staff-casework announcement of a new complaint name the member who filed it? *Today:* public.notify_action_request() posts to the casework staff channel: '**New case** <filer gamertag> filed a <label>. Subject: <subject up to 80 chars>. Open it: <link>'. It pings for complaints and appeals. It also names the filer in the commissioner and staff bell notices. The bot's department alert in bot/staff-alerts.mjs gives only the heading and does not name the filer. *Why it is settled:* 7.1.2 says what may be posted: 'only that one was filed, and under which heading'. The filer's name is neither, so the book settles it and the casework post is a MISMATCH to fix. The only way this becomes a question is if the commissioner chooses to amend the book to allow naming the filer.
- **Q-9-10-2** (Rule 9.2.1). Should the system refuse a ballot cast for the voter himself or for a player on his own club, or is that left to the voters' honor? *Today:* Nothing enforces the own-club bar. award_ballots has no triggers; its constraints are only a category CHECK and UNIQUE(season_id, category, voter_id). The RLS INSERT/UPDATE policies check only voter_id = auth.uid(), is_staff() or is_commissioner(), and not is_media_staff(). The Staff Desk picker (src/live/part_live.js:9987-9993) lists every player for MVP and Rookie of the Year, and every G or D for the position awards, including the voter himself. finalize_season_award does not screen ballots either. *Why it is settled:* Not a genuine decision. [9.2.1] already settles the rule in plain words: 'No voter may cast a first-place vote for a member of their own club.' What is left is an enforcement fix: add a trigger or policy check against roster_spots, filter the picker, and drop 'first-place', because each ballot holds one choice per award. The gap is live: 10 of the 11 current voters (3 of 3 commissioners, 7 of 8 staff) hold roster spots this season. No ballots have been cast yet (award_ballots count 0). This duplicates F-9-10-2, which is not in this batch.
- **Q-9-10-3** (Rule 9.2.1). Should the vote counts be published with each award, or only the winner? *Today:* finalize_season_award(uuid,text,uuid) writes awards.decided_by='ballot' with no stat_line or tally. It inserts one news row whose body reads '<name> wins <label>, decided by staff ballot.' with no counts. award_ballots SELECT RLS is is_staff() OR is_commissioner(), so only the office sees tallies, on the Staff Desk (part_live.js:10027-10041). The public Awards page (part5b_public2.js:83-95) shows the winner and 'staff ballot' only. *Why it is settled:* Not a genuine decision. [9.2.1] says 'The league office publishes voting results with the award announcements.' Read next to 'award announcements', 'voting results' can only mean the counts, so the book already requires publication and the system falls short. That makes it a fix, or a book change if the commissioner prefers secrecy. This duplicates F-9-10-3, which is not in this batch.

## Part 5. Rules the system enforces that the book does not state

Proposed clauses, grouped by the rule they belong in. Where a clause depends on one of your answers, it says which.

- **Rule 0.1.3** (low). When a season's movement deadline passes and no row exists for the next season, the flip creates one (upcoming, registration open) carrying over the current season's format, roster size, cap, weeks, nights, deadline week, management salaries and holidays. *Proposed:* Until the commissioner publishes a season's settings, it carries the settings of the season before it. *Mechanism:* public.flip_season_status() insert branch (cron season-status-flip every 10 minutes).
- **Rule 0.1.3 (and 10.1.3)** (medium). The commissioner can change the playoff floor, position lock, salary cap, deadline week and other settings at any point in the season; only the round count locks, at the first selection. *Proposed:* Depends on Q-0-2 (settled, Part 4) (see the two clauses there). *Mechanism:* seasons table, RLS 'commissioner writes seasons'; public.guard_season_format checks only roster_max against the format, the deadline week before the last week, and no pre-season date in basic. *Needs an answer first:* Q-0-2 (settled, Part 4).
- **Rule 0.1.4** (low). The Thursday 11:59 PM ET cutoff, the Saturday draft and the Wednesday puck drop are produced by the editor's spacing; the database accepts any dates a commissioner types. *Proposed:* The dates published for a season on the site govern; where they differ from the pattern described in this chapter, the published dates prevail. *Mechanism:* seasons.signup_deadline_at, draft_at and starts_at are written by the Control Center season editor (Auto-space); no database check relates them.
- **Rule 0.2.1 (and 2.6.1)** (medium). Owner applications for a season open when its registration opens (at the previous season's movement deadline) and are refused after that season's owner-application deadline: 8:00 PM ET on 9 September 2026 for Season 1 and 8:00 PM ET on Friday 22 January 2027 for Season 2. An applicant may withdraw at any time; commissioners are exempt. *Proposed:* Applications to own a club open when a season's registration opens and close at the owner-application deadline the league office publishes for that season; an application filed after the deadline is refused, and an applicant may withdraw his application at any time. *Mechanism:* trigger guard_owner_app_deadline_trg (public.guard_owner_app_deadline); seasons.owner_app_deadline; the owner-application page reads CG.regSeason.
- **Rule 0.3.full.3 (system note)** (low). The full-format unsigned-contract rule (dead money, voiding and suspension after an ownership change) runs in basic seasons too; it stays inert only because basic contracts are one season and are closed at rollover. *Proposed:* None needed; the system could skip the job when the season is basic. *Mechanism:* public.enforce_unsigned_contracts() (cron contract-enforcement, every 15 minutes).
- **Rule 0.5.2 and 2.8.7** (high). The draft ends itself the moment the last unskipped selection is made; any selection skipped earlier by an expired clock can no longer be used and is replaced by a random placement ten minutes later. *Proposed:* The draft concludes automatically when every selection has been made or skipped and none remains on the clock; a skipped selection not used by then is replaced under Rule 2.8. *Mechanism:* public._draft_advance(integer) sets draft_state.status = 'complete' when no pick is both unused and unskipped; public.draft_make_pick refuses when the status is not live or paused. *Needs an answer first:* Q44.
- **Rule 0.7.1 (office note)** (low). A season becomes active automatically at its starts_at timestamp (opening night's puck drop), whatever the state of the previous season or of the manual rollover; current_season_id() switches at that moment. *Proposed:* None needed for members. Office note: run start_next_season before the new season's draft, since the season goes active on its own at puck drop. *Mechanism:* public.flip_season_status(), first statement.
- **Rule 0.9.2** (low). The rollover is a manual commissioner step. It refuses while an earlier season is not complete; in a basic season it expires every non-management player contract from earlier seasons, writes an ownership baseline and posts a rollover transaction. *Proposed:* The league office effects the rollover between the final and the next draft; on it every player contract of the season just ended is closed. *Mechanism:* public.start_next_season(uuid), commissioner-only RPC, no cron.
- **Rule 1.3.1 (nearest in this chapter: 0.3, 0.4)** (low). Automatic one-time posts: registration open; draft-eligibility deadline within 24 hours; 'the draft class is set' after the deadline only if at least 8 registered (otherwise commissioners are told privately); draft tonight, puck drop tonight and playoffs tonight, each within 12 hours; full-format pre-season and free-agency posts. *Proposed:* The league office announces in the league Discord the opening of registration, the approach of the draft-eligibility deadline, draft night, opening night and the start of the playoffs; those announcements are courtesies and do not alter any date set by this rulebook. *Mechanism:* cron lifecycle-announcements */5 -> public.announce_lifecycle() -> public._announce_once() (at most once per season per key).
- **Rule 1.1.1** (medium). A commissioner can decline a registration, which removes the player from the draft pool and from every automatic placement while the row remains. The member is not notified by the system. No registration is declined today. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.1.1: "The league office may decline a registration for cause, including a ban or a member's request to withdraw after registration has closed. A declined player is neither drafted nor placed, and the league office tells him so." (The last clause is done by hand today.) *Mechanism:* season_registrations.status = 'declined' (commissioner RLS); excluded by is_draft_eligible, draft_make_pick and every placement function; no notification on the status change.
- **Rule 1.1.1** (low). A sign-up is refused unless the member is in the Discord server and has an EA ID, a console and a console name on file. A commissioner or trusted writer may register a member by hand without the three details; the Discord test applies to everyone. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.1.1: "The league office may enter a registration on a member's behalf and complete his details afterward." *Mechanism:* public.require_registration_details() (BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE on season_registrations).
- **Rule 1.1.1** (low). Every sign-up is posted in #season-signups with the member's Discord mention, EA ID, position and number, and the whole pool (gamertag, EA ID, position, sign-up time, waived flag) can be read by anyone, signed-out visitors included. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.1.1: "Each registration is announced in the league Discord's sign-ups channel with the player's EA ID, position and number, and the sign-up board, showing each registrant's gamertag, EA ID, position and date of registration, is public." *Mechanism:* trigger on_registration_notify_discord -> public.notify_discord_signup() (#season-signups, with a mention); public.registration_pool() executable by anon.
- **Rule 1.1.1 (nearest)** (low). Each new member is greeted once in #welcome by mention, instantly or within five minutes, after he passes the rules screen; more than 15 arrivals in one sweep are recorded without greeting. *Proposed:* None required. If wanted, add to Rule 1.1.1: "A member who joins the league Discord is welcomed in the welcome channel." *Mechanism:* netlify/functions/discord-welcome.js (every 5 minutes) and bot/handlers.mjs welcome; welcomed_members; BURST_CAP 15.
- **Rule 1.1.3** (medium). Any commissioner or staff member of any department may withdraw any sign-up at any time, including after the movement deadline. A rostered player's spot goes with it, his contract ends, and he and his club are notified. A player holding an Owner, GM or AGM seat is refused until he is removed from the seat. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.1.3: "The league office may withdraw a registered player from the season at his request or for cause. A rostered player so withdrawn leaves his club's roster and his contract ends. A player holding a seat in a club's front office must first be removed from it (Rule 2.6)." *Mechanism:* public.office_withdraw_registration(uuid,text), gated is_commissioner() or is_staff(); refuses a seat holder (Rule 2.6). *Needs an answer first:* Q11.
- **Rule 1.1.3 / 2.2** (medium). A commissioner may remove any non-manager player from a roster at any time; the removal is logged as 'League office removed PLAYER from CLUB' and the registration is reset to pending without waived_at. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 2.2: "The league office may remove a player from a club's roster. The removal is posted to the transaction log, and the player so removed [per Q12]." *Mechanism:* public.admin_remove_from_roster(uuid,uuid) (commissioner only; season not complete). *Needs an answer first:* Q12.
- **Rule 1.1.4** (low). The names of sign-ups withdrawn for leaving the server are posted in the public #member-departures channel, in addition to the site notification to the member. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.1.4: "The names of sign-ups withdrawn under this paragraph are posted in the league Discord's member departures channel." *Mechanism:* discord-sync.js removeDepartedSignups() -> public.remove_departed_signups(24); posts to #member-departures.
- **Rule 1.1.6 (nearest)** (low). A recurring sign-up reminder in #season-signups while registration is open; currently paused by the commissioner. *Proposed:* None while paused; the book should not promise a recurring sign-up reminder. *Mechanism:* discord-scheduler.js signupReminder() 6:00 to 6:09 PM ET every signup_reminder_days (3); app_config.signup_reminder_enabled = off.
- **Rule 1.3 (new 1.3.5)** (medium). Every member who leaves the server is posted within about two minutes (instantly when the gateway bot is up) in the public #member-departures channel, with how long he was a member and his league standing at that moment (club seat, roster or camp spot and club, staff department, or signed-up position). A drop of more than a quarter of the server in one sweep is recorded but not announced. *Proposed:* Add Rule 1.3.5: "When a member leaves the league Discord, the league posts in the member departures channel, which every member can read, how long he was a member and the place he held in the league at that moment, including any club, seat or roster spot." *Mechanism:* discord-sync.js trackDepartures()/announceDepartures(), bot/handlers.mjs instant lane, shared/departure-card.mjs, public.member_league_card; DEPART_SANITY 0.25.
- **Rule 1.3.1** (low). Automatic Discord posts: registration open; cutoff tomorrow (24 hours before); draft class set (only when at least 8 are registered, otherwise held and commissioners flagged); draft tonight, puck drop tonight and playoffs tonight (12 hours before each). Every site news item is mirrored to #news with its title, category and an excerpt, and any gamertag or club name in it becomes a mention. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.3.1: "The league announces the opening of registration, the sign-up cutoff, draft night, puck drop and the start of the playoffs in the Discord automatically, and league news published on the site is mirrored to the Discord's news channel." *Mechanism:* public.announce_lifecycle() (cron lifecycle-announcements, every 5 minutes); trigger on_news_notify_discord -> public.notify_discord_news().
- **Rule 1.3.3** (low). Every club role is mentionable by any member of the server in any channel he can post in, not only by that club's members or management. Discord offers no way to limit a role mention to the role's own members short of the server-wide mention permission. *Proposed:* Amend Rule 1.3.3: "Clubs reach their own members with their club role. Any member may mention a club's role; mentioning another club's role to harass or disrupt it is conduct subject to Chapter 7." *Mechanism:* discord-sync.js club role reconcile loop (patch.mentionable = true every sweep).
- **Rule 1.3.3 (nearest; also 2.6.1)** (low). A seat holder carries his seat's Discord role and the combined CGHL Management role for as long as he holds the seat. A Team Management category holds #owners-chat, #management-chat, #management-help, #management-moves (automatic seat changes), #management-announcements and #club-ids (read-only). #trade-block is visible only to management and commissioners; #free-agency to those plus Free Agents. Each club has a private room open to its players and the Commissioner and Staff roles; seat roles are stripped from other clubs' rooms. Reconciled every two minutes. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.3.2: "A member holding a club seat carries that seat's Discord role and the league's management role while he holds the seat. The league office reaches club management through the management channels of the Discord, and a notice posted in the management announcements channel binds every club's front office as a posting under Rule 1.3.1 binds every player. Each club has a private room open to its players and the league office." *Mechanism:* shared/roles.mjs desiredRolesFor (seat roles and 'CGHL Management'); discord-sync.js MGMT_ROOMS (Team Management category) and CHANNEL_AUDIENCE for #trade-block and #free-agency; ensureClubRooms.
- **Rule 1.3.4** (low). #transactions and #game-scores are feeds members read but cannot post or thread in; every channel in the Information category is read-only for members (commissioners may post). #scouting-links is covered by 1.3.4 already. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 1.3.4: "The channels of the Information category, the transactions channel and the game scores channel are read-only for members and carry the league office's announcements and the automatic feeds." *Mechanism:* discord-sync.js enforcePostOnlyBoards() (POST_ONLY_FEEDS transactions, game-scores) and enforceReadOnlyCategories() (Information category).
- **Rule 1.3.4** (medium). A mention-heavy message from a non-exempt member is blocked and, as configured at the anti-spam baseline, the member is timed out for ten minutes automatically. Whether the timeout action is still configured cannot be read from the repository or database. *Proposed:* If the timeout is confirmed in the Discord AutoMod settings, add to Rule 1.3.4: "A message that mentions an excessive number of members or roles is refused, and the member who posted it is automatically timed out for ten (10) minutes; the timeout is a spam control and not a sanction under Chapter 7." *Mechanism:* AutoMod 'Mention spam' rule actions (hand-configured 2026-08-08 per memory/discord-automations.md; the sweep reconciles only its exemptions).
- **Rule 10.2 (routed from 1.3.1)** (low). Two FAQ forums hold the league's guides; only Commissioner and Staff may open a post; members may reply and react; the management forum is visible to front offices only. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 10.2: "The league's guides and FAQ posts explain the rules; where a guide and this rulebook differ, the rulebook governs." *Mechanism:* discord-sync.js ensureFaqForums() (#management-faq, #player-faq; FAQ_MEMBER_ALLOW/DENY).
- **Rule 3.1.1 (routed from 1.3.1)** (medium). Each Tuesday at 5:00 PM ET the coming six days' unplayed games are posted by day with times and up to three featured matchups; club roles render as pills but nobody is pinged. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 3.1.1: "The league posts each game week's schedule in the league Discord at 5:00 PM Eastern Time on the Tuesday before it; the schedule on the site governs where the two differ." *Mechanism:* discord-scheduler.js weeklySchedule(): Tuesday 5:00 to 5:09 PM ET; discord_schedule_webhook; claim weekly_schedule.
- **Rule 6.1 (routed from 1.3.1)** (low). Every result is announced in #game-scores: final (with OT), forfeit, voided, corrected, reversed, and Rule 4.3 forfeits. A change of the overtime flag alone, a kept-score forfeit ruled by statistics staff, and a re-opened final are not announced. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 6.1: "Every result, forfeit, void, correction and reversal is announced in the league's game scores channel when it is entered." *Mechanism:* public.notify_discord_game_final() (on_game_final_notify_discord), undo_forfeit, unforfeit_game, forfeit_abandoned_game posting to discord_scores_webhook.
- **Rule 1.3.1 (with 2.2.3, 2.3.1)** (low). Every transaction-log entry is mirrored to #transactions and notifies the named players and clubs. *Proposed:* Each entry in the transaction log is mirrored to the league Discord's transactions channel as it is recorded, and the players and clubs it names are notified there. *Mechanism:* notify_discord_transaction (pings users and roles).
- **Rule 2.1** (low). Numbers run 1 to 99, unique within a club for the season; management sets them; a new player receives the lowest free number; a traded player keeps his unless taken. *Proposed:* Each rostered player wears a number from one (1) to ninety-nine (99), unique within his club for the season. Club management sets its players' numbers; a player joining a club receives its lowest unused number until management sets another, and a player acquired by trade keeps his number unless his new club already uses it. *Mechanism:* public.set_jersey_number; unique (season_id, team_id, jersey_number); CHECK 1..99; lowest free number on arrival.
- **Rule 2.1.4** (medium). A new roster entry defaults to the active roster; if the group or total is full he goes to training camp instead of being refused, while camp has room. *Proposed:* Where a player joins a club by selection, signing or placement and the club's active roster, or his position group within it, is already full, he is entered in the club's training camp rather than refused, and management may call him up when the active roster has room. *Mechanism:* public.place_new_roster_spot() BEFORE INSERT on roster_spots. *Needs an answer first:* Q2.
- **Rule 2.1.4** (low). A one-step swap must exchange players of the same position group, even with position locks lifted. *Proposed:* A single exchange of an active player for a camp player is made between players of the same position group; an exchange across groups is made as a send-down and a call-up, each within the published composition. *Mechanism:* public.swap_roster_squad requires the same pos_group.
- **Rule 2.2** (medium). Every removal is archived with reason and actor; the office can restore the player to the same club, position, squad, salary and (if free) number, revive his contract, clear waived_at and post a reinstatement, subject to cap and composition. *Proposed:* The league office keeps a record of every removal from a roster. Where a removal was made in error or without authority, the office may reverse it, restoring the player to the club, position, squad and salary he held and, where it is free, to the number he wore; a reversal is posted to the transaction log and is subject to the composition of Rule 2.1 and the cap of Rule 2.5 as they then stand. *Mechanism:* archive_roster_spot (AFTER DELETE) into roster_spot_removals (read: commissioner or staff); public.reinstate_roster_spot (commissioner or staff).
- **Rule 2.2.3** (low). A waiver expires the contract (salary kept on it), voids any future contract, withdraws that club's pending offers, stamps waived_at, posts to the log, notifies the seats, the club room and the player, and resyncs his Discord role. *Proposed:* The waived player is notified on the site, and his club's Discord role is withdrawn. *Mechanism:* public.waive_player effects; contract_on_waive; archive_roster_spot; notify_roster_remove.
- **Rule 2.2.4** (medium). A suspended or banned player cannot be signed, offered a contract, drafted, placed or added to a roster except by a commissioner. A rostered player who is suspended keeps his spot, contract and salary against the cap, and may be traded (the insert-only trigger does not fire on a trade). *Proposed:* No club may sign or select, and the league office shall not place, a player who is under suspension or banned. A rostered player who is suspended keeps his roster spot, contract and salary, which continues to count against his club's cap, and is ineligible to dress under Rule 5.2 until the suspension ends; he may be traded, and the unserved games follow him. (This replaces the last sentence of Rule 2.5.4, since nothing is reinstated.) *Mechanism:* trg_block_suspended_roster (BEFORE INSERT), block_suspended_contract, checks in sign_free_agent and respond_offer; suspend_player leaves roster and contract untouched.
- **Rule 2.3.1** (low). An offer stands until answered or withdrawn; several may name the same player; an offer cannot be edited; a counter is a new offer that declines the one it answers; at acceptance any moved player makes the whole offer stale. *Proposed:* An offer stands until the receiving club accepts or declines it or the offering club withdraws it, and it cannot be changed once sent. A club answers an offer with a counter by sending a new offer of its own, which declines the offer it answers. At acceptance each player in the offer must still be held by the club offering him; an offer in which any player has moved is refused in full. *Mechanism:* No expiry or count limit on trades; accept_trade staleness check; guard_trade_assets freezes assets; counter declines the original.
- **Rule 2.3.1** (low). A traded player's contract and salary pass unchanged to the acquiring club. *Proposed:* A traded player's contract and salary pass unchanged to the club that acquires him and count against its cap from the moment the trade is accepted. *Mechanism:* sync_contract_team moves the contract; move_player leaves salary unchanged. *Needs an answer first:* Q53.
- **Rule 2.3.1** (medium). The database accepts an offer that sends or receives nothing, so a one-sided transfer can be made through the API. *Proposed:* Each side of a trade shall include at least one player; an offer that sends or receives nothing is refused. (The database should enforce it.) *Mechanism:* trades has only CHECK from_team_id <> to_team_id; the client requires a player each way.
- **Rule 2.3.1** (low). A completed trade is posted publicly, announced in both club rooms, and each traded player is notified. *Proposed:* A completed trade is announced in the league's transactions channel and in both clubs' rooms, and each player who changes clubs is notified on the site. *Mechanism:* accept_trade inserts a transactions row (posted to #transactions with mentions); notify_trade_status posts both rooms; 'You were traded' notification.
- **Rule 2.3.3** (medium). A traded player always lands on the receiving club's active roster; if that breaches the composition the whole trade is refused, although camp has room. *Proposed:* A trade is refused at acceptance if a player it brings in would carry the receiving club's active roster beyond the published composition; the receiving club makes room first by moving a player to training camp, which it cannot do while the weekly freeze of Rule 2.1 is in force. *Mechanism:* reset_squad_on_team_change sets squad 'pro'; deferred check_roster_structure.
- **Rule 2.3.3** (medium). A reversal needs a written reason, which is published; only one seat per club receives it directly; returning players are not notified and go to the active roster; the cap is not re-examined. *Proposed:* A reversal is made with written reasons, which are published with it in the transaction log and given to every member of both management groups; each player returned to his former club is notified and joins its active roster, subject to the composition of Rule 2.1. A trade voided under Rule 2.3.2 is undone by the same reversal. (The system should give all three seats the reason and notify the players.) *Mechanism:* public.reverse_trade(uuid,text): reason of at least ten characters; reason in the public log; reason sent to team_primary_contact only; no notice to the players; retention left in place. *Needs an answer first:* Q36.
- **Rule 2.3.3** (low). Both clubs and the transactions desk see an automatic 'Even' or 'Favors' reading of every offer; it blocks nothing. *Proposed:* The trade balance shown in the Trade Hub is an informational estimate; it is not a ruling, and a trade's review under this paragraph rests on the department's own judgment. *Mechanism:* CG.tradeBalanceCard / CG.tradeValue (client).
- **Rule 2.3.5, 2.6.2** (low). Any seat may list or delist, under the Owner's Roster page access (not the Trade Hub); a listing can be made after the deadline and is still posted. *Proposed:* A trade-block listing is a move on the roster page and follows the access set for that page. A manager, or any player after the movement deadline, may not be listed, and every listing ends when the movement deadline passes. (The system should enforce and clear it.) *Mechanism:* RLS 'gm updates own team roster'; trg_mgmt_gate_block gates on_block under the 'roster' page; no guard for managers or after the deadline.
- **Rule 2.4.1** (medium). A commissioner can close all movement early or hold it open past the deadline; the same switch decides when the next season's registration opens. The deadline is computed from the last game day of the deadline week. *Proposed:* The commissioner may, by published notice, close player movement before the deadline or reopen it after the deadline; the notice states the period and the moves it permits, and registration for the following season opens when movement closes. Where the deadline week's last game is played on a day other than Friday, movement closes at midnight Eastern at the end of that day. *Mechanism:* seasons.moves_lock_override ('auto','locked','open') read by public.moves_locked(uuid); flip_season_status keys on it.
- **Rule 2.4.1** (low). After the deadline no club can sign anyone; only a commissioner can put a player on a roster, at the league minimum. *Proposed:* An emergency goaltender signing after the deadline is made by the league office on the club's behalf, at the league minimum salary, and is posted to the transaction log. *Mechanism:* sign_free_agent requires a seat; Control Center CG.assignRegistration (commissioner insert, salary floored to $750,000).
- **Rule 2.4.1** (medium). An offer pending at the deadline can no longer be declined or withdrawn by either club and stays 'proposed' indefinitely. *Proposed:* An offer that has not been accepted when the movement deadline passes lapses at the deadline and can no longer be accepted, declined or withdrawn. (The system should then close such offers automatically.) *Mechanism:* guard_trade_insert fires on UPDATE OF status and applies moves_locked to non-commissioners; no job closes offers.
- **Rule 2.5.2** (low). Once written a salary can be changed only by a commissioner or the seat and retention machinery; the lattice check does not cover commissioner writes. *Proposed:* A player's salary is fixed when he is drafted, placed or signed and may be altered during the season only by a commissioner correcting an error, or as Rule 2.6.3 provides; the league office records no salary off the lattice other than the fixed management values of Rule 2.6. *Mechanism:* block_noncommish_salary (BEFORE UPDATE OF salary) with app.mgr_sync and app.retention_sync doors; legal_salary on offers.
- **Rule 2.6.1** (medium). Seating a player rostered on another club moves him and his contract to the seating club with no trade, cap or deadline check and no roster notice. *Proposed:* A player rostered on one club may be seated in another club's front office only by the league office. The appointment moves him to that club, removes his salary from his former club's payroll, and is posted to the transaction log and announced to both clubs. *Mechanism:* ensure_manager_rostered moves an existing spot and contract under app.mgr_sync. *Needs an answer first:* Q52.
- **Rule 2.6.1** (medium). Only a commissioner seats or unseats directly; seating in a held seat displaces the holder, who is told afterwards. *Proposed:* The power to seat and unseat management directly is exercised by a commissioner alone. A commissioner may seat a member in a held seat, which vacates it for its holder by the same act; a member so seated who held another club seat vacates that seat, and each person displaced is notified. *Mechanism:* public.set_team_manager / _set_team_seat (commissioner only; replaces a sitting holder; empties the member's other seat; writes the transaction log). *Needs an answer first:* Q-2-10 (settled, Part 4).
- **Rule 2.6.1** (low). Every seat change is posted to #management-moves with mentions; an appointment pings the club role; office seatings enter the log but reviewer-approved appointments do not. *Proposed:* Every appointment to and departure from a front-office seat is announced in the league's management channel, and an appointment is announced to the club's members; every such change, however made, is entered in the transaction log. (The reviewer path must write the log entry it now omits.) *Mechanism:* teams_mgmt_move triggers; flush-mgmt-moves cron; reviewer path writes no transaction row.
- **Rule 2.6.1** (medium). Reviewers are the Applications department; an application is decided when every reviewer has voted, by a majority of all reviewers (a tie denies); ballots may change until decided; a commissioner may override; a change in reviewers re-tests pending applications. *Proposed:* The reviewers are the members of the league office assigned to the Applications department. An application is decided when every reviewer has cast a ballot and is approved only by a majority of all reviewers; an equal division denies it. A reviewer may change his ballot until the application is decided, and a commissioner may decide any application in place of the vote. Where the department's membership changes while an application is pending, it is decided on the ballots of the reviewers then serving. *Mechanism:* application_ballots, resolve_application_if_ready, app_reviewer_count, override_application_decision, trg_profiles_resolve. *Needs an answer first:* Q47.
- **Rule 2.6.1** (medium). Owner applications open with the next season's registration and close at that season's owner_app_deadline; applicants may withdraw at any time. *Proposed:* Applications to own a club for a season open when that season's registration opens and close at the time the league office publishes for that season; an applicant may withdraw at any time. *Mechanism:* guard_owner_app_deadline; CG.regSeason.
- **Rule 2.6.1** (low). A nomination notifies commissioners, Applications staff and the nominee; the filing Owner (or any staff member) may withdraw it; applications and their messages are readable by the applicant, staff (Media excluded for owner and staff applications) and commissioners. *Proposed:* A member nominated to a club seat is notified when the nomination is filed and when it is decided. The Owner who filed a nomination may withdraw it before it is decided. An application and the correspondence on it are confidential to the applicant (or, for a nomination, the Owner and the nominee) and the league office. *Mechanism:* notify_mgmt_application; RLS 'mgmt app update' (Owner or any staff may withdraw); application read policies.
- **Rule 2.6.2** (low). Managers are notified whenever the Owner changes their access; a queued move stays pending until decided, withdrawn or superseded. *Proposed:* A manager whose access the Owner changes is notified of the change and of the access he now holds. A queued move remains pending until the Owner decides it or the manager withdraws or replaces it; it lapses in no other way. *Mechanism:* set_team_mgmt_policy notifies both managers; team_mgmt_moves has no expiry. *Needs an answer first:* Q48.
- **Rule 2.6.3** (low). Management cap values are per-season settings, forced onto seated managers and re-applicable to every seat. *Proposed:* The cap values of management contracts are season settings, determined by the commissioner and published with the settings of Rule 2.1 before the draft; for the current season they are $0 for the Owner and the General Manager and $2,000,000 for the Assistant General Manager. *Mechanism:* seasons.owner_salary/gm_salary/agm_salary; mgmt_salary_of; apply_mgmt_salaries; flip_season_status copies forward.
- **Rule 2.6.3** (medium). A GM or AGM cannot step down himself; an Owner cannot step down except through a commissioner. *Proposed:* A General Manager or Assistant General Manager who wishes to step down asks the Owner to remove him, and an Owner who wishes to step down asks the league office to vacate his seat; the consequences of this paragraph follow in each case. *Mechanism:* No step-down RPC or button for a seat holder.
- **Rule 2.7.2** (low). A staff application names at most two departments; an approval that would breach Rule 2.7 grants no role and the applicant is told; a staffer cannot add departments to himself (the call silently reverts); Media-only staff see no club or staff rooms, cannot read applications, cannot vote. *Proposed:* An application to join the league staff names no more than two departments, and a member holding a club seat may apply only for Media. An approval that would breach this rule is not given effect until the conflict is resolved, and the applicant is told why. A staff member's departments are those granted on his application or assigned by a commissioner. A staff member on Media alone has the access his department requires to report on the league and no access to the office's casework, applications or private club rooms. *Mechanism:* staff_app_department_cap; apply_application_decision staff branch; guard_profile_role silent revert; shared/roles.mjs. *Needs an answer first:* Q50.
- **Rule 2.8.1** (medium). The clock is two minutes unless changed; expiry is acted on at the next minute tick, so a pick made in that gap stands. *Proposed:* The selection clock is two (2) minutes unless the league office announces another figure before the draft; a selection made before the league records the expiry stands. *Mechanism:* start_draft default 120 seconds; draft_reset_clock 15 to 600; draft-watchdog cron every minute; draft_make_pick does not test clock_ends_at.
- **Rule 2.8.1** (medium). A selection is refused if it would take the club beyond the published composition (management counted). *Proposed:* No selection may take a club beyond the composition published under Rule 2.1; the draft room refuses such a selection, and the club selects a player of another position group. *Mechanism:* public.draft_fits in draft_make_pick and _place_for_unused_picks.
- **Rule 2.8.1** (low). The club on the clock is notified on the site and pinged in the draft channel; every selection is posted to Discord. *Proposed:* Each selection, and each club as it comes on the clock, is announced in the league Discord's draft channel, and the club on the clock is notified there and on the site. *Mechanism:* _draft_notify_on_clock; notify_discord_draft_pick.
- **Rule 2.8.4, 1.1.3** (medium). A member who withdraws and re-registers after the cutoff loses eligibility; the office can restore the date; with no cutoff set, everyone is eligible. *Proposed:* A player who withdraws and registers again is treated as registering on the later date for the purpose of the sign-up cutoff, unless the league office restores his original date. *Mechanism:* is_draft_eligible keys on the current registration's created_at; guard_registration_columns lets office write it.
- **Rule 2.8.6** (low). The office may assign each registrant a scouting rating visible to club management before the draft. *Proposed:* The league office may publish a scouting rating for each registrant; it is advisory and binds no club. *Mechanism:* season_registrations.scout_ovr (office writes, managers read).
- **Rule 2.8.8** (medium). The unused-selection pass draws only from players registered by the cutoff. It counts replacements currently on the club with origin postdraft_random and does not test waived_at, so a club that trades or waives its replacement receives another, possibly the same waived player. Latent: no Season 1 club had an unused selection. *Proposed:* Amend the first pass of Rule 2.8.8 to 'chosen at random from the players registered by the sign-up cutoff and still without a club'. The one-for-one replacement at conclusion is the intended rule; the system should count replacements ever made and skip waived players. *Mechanism:* distribute_unproven_rookies and _place_for_unused_picks (cron every two minutes).
- **Rule 2.9.2** (low). A commissioner may change a position at any time; it rewrites roster and registered position, posts to the log, must fit the composition, and recalculates the overall rating. *Proposed:* A position changed by the league office changes both the registered position and the roster position, is posted to the transaction log, must leave the club within the published composition, and recalculates the player's overall rating at his new position. *Mechanism:* set_player_position (commissioner, any time); trg_roster_pos_overall.
- **Rule 5.3 (with 2.6.2)** (medium). Only the club's seats, within the Owner's access, or a commissioner may file a club's lineup; a commissioner may file, change or withdraw any club's sheet. *Proposed:* A club's lineup is filed by its Owner, General Manager or Assistant General Manager, within the access the Owner has granted under Rule 2.6. A commissioner may file, change or withdraw a lineup for any club in the exercise of the league office's authority, and every such action is recorded against the commissioner who took it. *Mechanism:* set_game_lineup permission block (is_commissioner or is_gm_of; mgmt_gate 'lines'; suspended refused).
- **Rule 3.2 (new 3.2.7)** (high). A staff member rostered on, or managing, one of the two clubs can log incidents, rule a forfeit, reverse one, or correct the score of his own club's game. *Proposed:* No member of league staff may log an incident, enter or reverse a forfeit, or correct the result of a game in which he plays, or which involves a club he manages. *Mechanism:* declare_forfeit, forfeit_game, undo_forfeit, unforfeit_game, log_game_incident, stats_game_set_result: none checks whether the caller plays for or manages either club. *Needs an answer first:* Q21.
- **Rule 3.2.4** (medium). When a ten-minute lateness is logged, both clubs are told in their rooms that the forfeit is not waivable, and staff see the same words. *Proposed:* Depends on the answer: either 'The two clubs may not waive it between themselves; only a member of league staff who is not involved in the game may.' (book stands, copy changes) or strike the waiver sentence. *Mechanism:* public.log_game_incident ruling text; bot/incidents.mjs ruling headline and club-room footer. *Needs an answer first:* Q-3-1 (settled, Part 4).
- **Rule 3.2.6** (low). Nothing tallies a club's forfeits or refers repeated forfeits; a referral happens only if staff notice. *Proposed:* A club that forfeits [N] games in a season is referred to the officiating department for review under Chapter 7. *Mechanism:* No automated count of forfeits per club in staff_briefing, weekly_staff_report or _staff_attention. *Needs an answer first:* Q42.
- **Rule 3.3.1** (medium). A game moved into another calendar week still counts toward the appearance cap and availability sheet of the week it was originally scheduled in. *Proposed:* A rescheduled game counts toward the appearance cap of [the week it was originally scheduled in / the week in which it is played]. *Mechanism:* public.ops_reschedule_game updates scheduled_at only; games.week unchanged; player_week_games counts by games.week. *Needs an answer first:* Q40.
- **Rule 3.3.1** (low). There is no place on the site for a club to file a reschedule request or the other club's agreement; the request reaches the office by Discord or ticket, and only the move itself is recorded. *Proposed:* The request is filed by one club's management with the operations department, with the other club's written agreement attached. *Mechanism:* action_requests_type_check allows only complaint, appeal, trade_request, position_change; no reschedule request form or RPC.
- **Rule 3.3.1** (low). A reschedule entered from the Operations desk is announced in #transactions, pinging both club roles; it is not posted to the club rooms, creates no site notification, and does not appear in the site's transactions list (which shows roster moves only). A commissioner move from the Control Center is not announced at all (F-3-9). *Proposed:* A rescheduled game is announced to both clubs in the league's Discord, with its new date and time. *Mechanism:* ops_reschedule_game inserts a transactions row of type 'other'; notify_discord_transaction posts it to #transactions with both clubs' Discord roles mentioned.
- **Rule 4.2.3** (medium). The roster is read as it stands when the check runs, not as it stood at puck drop, and a finding is a referral that officiating decides; a rename undoes a clearance. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 4.2.3: "A report under this paragraph is a referral, not a ruling. The officiating department decides it, having regard to the club's roster as it stood at puck drop, and may clear it where the player was properly held at that time." *Mechanism:* public.review_game_records finding unrostered_player: one notice per game to officiating and the casework channel; dedup on admin_audit; cleared only by a hand-written 'unrostered_cleared' audit row keyed on the gamertag.
- **Rule 4.2.7** (low). Server picks for every game of a night freeze 30 minutes before the night's first puck drop; after that only a commissioner can change them. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 4.2.7: "After the freeze only a commissioner may alter a club's picks." *Mechanism:* trigger trg_guard_veto_deadline calling public.guard_veto_deadline(): refuses insert or update after night_lock_at(game) unless is_commissioner().
- **Rule 4.3.10** (medium). The moment an incident is logged, the offending club's room is told what it owes and the other club's room is told that the choice of how two penalties are served is theirs. The late-start copy says 'The ten-minute forfeit is not waivable', although Rule 3.2.4 lets an uninvolved member of league staff waive it. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 4.3.10: "Each entry in the game incident log is announced at once in the club room of each club in the game: the offending club is told the penalties it owes, and, where two are owed, the other club is told that the manner of serving them is its choice." Correct the bot copy (bot/incidents.mjs:183) to "The ten-minute forfeit cannot be waived by the clubs." *Mechanism:* bot/incidents.mjs (Supabase Realtime on game_incidents, 24-hour catch-up, one claim per room) posting to both clubs' rooms.
- **Rule 4.3.10** (low). Every signed-in member can read the whole incident log for every game, including staff notes and the reporter. *Proposed:* If open: "The game incident log is open to every member of the league." If not: no clause; narrow the policy. *Mechanism:* RLS game_incidents_read (authenticated, using true). *Needs an answer first:* Q58.
- **Rule 4.3.3** (medium). A replay merges automatically only if it ends inside the fixture's window, within three hours of the previous sitting, with neither club having played anyone else in between, and no longer than 90 minutes of game clock. Any other case is merged by hand. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 4.3.3: "Sittings are combined automatically when the replay ends inside the fixture's game window, within three (3) hours of the previous sitting, and neither club has played another opponent in between. Any other case is combined by the statistics department or the clubs' management under Rule 6.2." *Mechanism:* netlify/functions/ingest-stats.js ingestContinuation: RESUME_WINDOW_S 3 h, SITTING_CAP_S 5400 s, no different opponent in between; mergeSegments sums every statistic; an ambiguous candidate goes to statistics staff.
- **Rule 4.3.3** (medium). A combined game is published as final even when its sittings together are still short of a full game, although a single short sitting is held. *Proposed:* Depends on Q18. If held: "A combined game is published as a result once its sittings together make a full game or it has been decided in overtime; until then it is held." *Mechanism:* ingest-stats.js ingestContinuation and leagueMerge set status 'final' with no minimum combined clock. *Needs an answer first:* Q18.
- **Rule 4.3.9** (medium). A game whose first sitting was short of regulation is held unpublished; if nothing more arrives within three hours of the scheduled puck drop, the statistics desk and the commissioners are flagged once, and the game stays unplayed on the schedule until someone rules. *Proposed:* Under Q-4-4 (settled, Part 4) option B, add to Rule 4.3.9: "A game whose record shows less than a full regulation game is held and not published as a result until the rest of it is combined. Where nothing further arrives within three (3) hours of the scheduled puck drop, the statistics department rules on it under this paragraph." *Mechanism:* discord-sync.js calling public.review_held_games(3), which calls notify_department('statistics', ...) once per game. *Needs an answer first:* Q-4-4 (settled, Part 4).
- **Rule 5.1.1** (medium). Only the player can enter or change his own availability. His club's management and the league office can read it on the site; teammates cannot. But every answer and every change is posted to the club's room with his per-night counts and the first 60 characters of each nightly note, so the whole club sees the notes. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 5.1.1: "Availability is entered by the player himself; neither his club nor the league office enters it for him. Each answer, and each change to it, is posted in his club's room together with any note he attaches, so a note is visible to every player on the club and should be written accordingly." *Mechanism:* RLS on availability (insert and update only where profile_id = auth.uid(); read by self, commissioners, staff and the player's club management); trigger notify_availability_submitted_trg inserts a club_notices row per submission and per change.
- **Rule 5.1.4** (low). A suspended or banned rostered player is never reminded, nudged or named as missing availability. *Proposed:* Add to Rule 5.1.4: "A player under suspension is not asked for availability for the weeks in which he cannot dress." *Mechanism:* public.availability_missing excludes is_suspended() and is_banned() profiles; it feeds both the 24-hour reminder and availability_nudge_tick.
- **Rule 5.1.4** (low).
